# Code health ledger

The code and architecture must stay clean enough that the long roadmap (V customisation, then body, then world tools) keeps moving forward instead of looping on bug fixes. This ledger drives threshold-triggered deep reviews. Routine merge gates (tests, typechecks, [architecture boundary tests](architecture-contract.md), link check, coordinator diff read) run on every merge regardless.

## Review triggers

Run `python tools/review_due.py` after each merge into `main`. A deep review is due when **any** of these holds since the last reviewed commit below:

- **Merges:** 5 or more merged feature branches.
- **Changed lines:** 4,000 or more lines of TypeScript/Python source under `projects/` (tests and generated files excluded).
- **New subsystem:** a new top-level module family or host capability landed. Record it by hand in the table below.
- **Boundary exception:** a new exception was added to the [UI architecture boundary](ui-architecture-boundary.md).
- **Release:** a release tag is about to be created.

## How a deep review runs

- **Reviewers:** independent, read-only reviewers work in parallel by area, typically (1) domain and application core, (2) pipeline, resolver and host adapters, and (3) presentation and desktop.
- **Checks:** conformance to the architecture contract, duplication and dead code, coupling and layering, complexity hotspots, test gaps, error and cancellation handling, and whether new code extends cleanly to later domains.
- **Findings:** each is recorded below with a severity.
  - **High:** a correctness bug, a broken or bypassed boundary, a security/data-loss risk, or a design that would force rework of later features.
  - **Medium:** maintainability debt that will slow the next features.
  - **Low:** polish.

## Debt budget

Reviews never block feature work directly. Fixes run as a parallel cleanup track. **If more than 3 High findings are open, feature merges pause** until the count is back to 3 or fewer; cleanup and critical-path fixes may still merge.

## Open findings

| ID | Severity | Area | Finding | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| PREV-129 | **High** | Hair physics (host) | A small hostile animgraph can freeze the host: `compileDangleSpec`'s constraint walk (`dangle-spec.ts:222-253`) bounds depth (8) and counts only leaf constraints (2048), so nested `animDyngConstraintMulti` groups that reference one group many times are walked once per path (fan-out F costs F⁸ visits), on the host's main thread in `serveDangle` for every hairstyle mod with an animated component (reproduced: fan-out 8 took 16.8 M visits and 183 ms; fan-out 32 is about 1e12 visits from under 300 handles). Fix: refuse a group reached twice as a loop and count every visit against a budget | **Fixed** (claude/cleanup-motion; see below) |
| SAVE-11 | **High** | Save Explorer | Package decode expands its input about 130 times: `MAX_VALUES` allows 1,000,000 values per chunk (`engines/red-object/package.ts:44`), each struct allocates three objects plus an unused `fieldTypes` record (`:279`), and `readSavedLoadout` keeps every `EquipmentSystemPlayerData` chunk at once (`save-loadout.ts:51`); with LZ4's 255× expansion a crafted save of about 1 MB suffices (reproduced: a 7.6 MB package with 4 such chunks peaked at 0.8 GB in `readSavedV`). Fix: a values budget per reading, like `NameBudget`, and keep only the player's chunk | **Fixed** (claude/cleanup-save-native; see below) |
| SAVE-12 | **High** | Save Explorer | Chunk tables expand about 58 times in what the explorer keeps: a `{type,start,end}` object per chunk (`package.ts:148-152`), a row per chunk and an error string per failed chunk (`features/save-explorer/explorer.ts:166-183`), and the background check runs it on every package (`actions.ts:278-285`) (reproduced: 20 packages of 65,536 one-byte chunks, 11.3 MB, cost +652 MB). Fix: a chunk budget per save and compact rows | **Fixed** (claude/cleanup-save-native; see below) |
| PREV-130 | Med | Hair physics (solver) | Finite but extreme spec values drive the solver to NaN or Infinity (compile and parse check only finiteness, `dangle-spec.ts:205-323`), and the NaN persists until reset, so the hair part vanishes while physics is on (reproduced: ellipsoid `constraintScale1 = 0` at frame 1, `dangle-solver.ts:339-346`; `mass = 1e-320` overflows `damping/m`, `:226-227`; `gravity = 1e200`; link bounds `1e307`; `pull = 1e4, mass = 0.01` past the explicit-integration stability bound at frame 78). Fix: range limits at compile and parse, plus a non-finite guard in `frame` that resets to the input pose with a note | **Fixed** (claude/cleanup-motion; see below) |
| PREV-131 | Med | Creator lighting (shadows) | The studio key and rim shadow maps are disposed and never redrawn when preview quality notifies without a size change: `studio-light-rig.ts:43-48` disposes both maps unconditionally, `browser-head-attachment.ts:125` calls `setShadowQuality` on every quality notify, the cache key is unchanged, so `needsUpdate` stays false and Three binds its empty shadow texture: key and rim read fully shadowed on a still V until something moves (confirmed by reading) | Fixed (claude/lighting-setups) |
| SAVE-13 | Med | Save Explorer | Each background-check step publishes a revision and the panel rebuilds, `structuredClone`s and walks the whole tree (`actions.ts:284`, `view/panel.ts:77,308-314`) (reproduced: a 1 MB save with 20,000 empty package nodes stalls 220 ms per step, about 73 minutes in all). Fix: throttle publishing or invalidate single rows | **Fixed** (claude/cleanup-save-native; see below) |
| NATIVE-65 | Med | Native reader | `static:N` arrays take their count from the file instead of N (`native/red-values.ts:160-168`), so a vertex layout can hold thousands of elements; every `PS_TexCoord` element is decoded though `toGltf` uses two (`mesh-blob.ts:207,307`), and the estimate ignores stride (`mesh-glb.ts:324-327`): the real ceiling is about 8× the 128 MB buffer cap in the decode worker (reproduced: a 96 MB buffer with 2,000 UV elements estimated 7.7 MB, cost +315 MB) | **Fixed** (claude/cleanup-save-native; see below) |
| UI-117 | Med | Component library | TreeView's empty message sits below the visible area: `.tree-spacer { min-height: 100% }` (`public/studio.css:308`) pushes it to the tree's bottom edge (`components/tree-view.ts:63-64`) (reproduced in Chrome) | Fixed in claude/cleanup-components (see below) |
| UI-118 | Med | Component library | TreeView has no ResizeObserver and repaints only on `update()` and scroll (`tree-view.ts:65,106-109`), so a taller tree shows blank space (reproduced: after growing to 800 px, 16 of 29 visible rows drawn) | Fixed in claude/cleanup-components (see below) |
| UI-119 | Med | Component library | Focus is lost when the focused row leaves the list: `paint()` removes the element and `update()` picks a new target without focusing it (`tree-view.ts:81,114,119`) (reproduced with F on a Favourites row in Poses; the star and SearchField-Down paths fail the same way by reading) | Fixed in claude/cleanup-components (see below) |
| UI-120 | Med | Dock | A column of collapsed strips condensed during a shrink stays icon-only after the window grows back: each strip's length follows its tabs (`dock/dock-view.ts:195`) and `HeaderFitter`/`TabStrip.fit` size from that current length (reproduced 900 → 240 → 900 px; only a full re-render recovers) | Fixed in claude/cleanup-components (see below) |
| PREV-132 | Low | Creator lighting (shadows) | Shadow maps are not redrawn after a WebGL context restore (`scene-host.ts:195-199`, `lighting-preset-stage.ts:53,138-141`); a still V shows empty shadow textures until something moves (PREV-62's class) | Fixed (claude/lighting-setups) |
| PREV-133 | Low | Hair physics (seek) | Re-framing the camera resets the hair simulation (`head-rig.ts:227-236` seeks 0 then `time`, each running `resetDangles`, `idle-animation.ts:330-335,399-408`), and seeks cost far more than planned because posing the body dominates (reproduced: a 72 mm tip jump; 56 ms for a 20 s clip, 142 ms for 60 s, about 1.4 s at the 600 s cap). Fix: measure the offset without seeking, checkpoint per loop | **Fixed** (claude/cleanup-motion; see below) |
| PREV-134 | Low | Idle intro | The face intro's timing depends on history: `faceStart` is set in `setClips` (`idle-animation.ts:264-268`) and never by `seek`, so a seek before it holds the intro's first frame (`:483-490`); the bake also samples the loop at `t mod D` during the intro and `(t − intro) mod D` after (`bake_idle_face.py:181,184`), resuming 4 s out of phase with the body clip | **Fixed** (claude/cleanup-motion; see below) |
| PREV-135 | Low | Creator lighting (workspace) | The untouched-defaults token isn't a clean round trip (reproduced: an explicitly chosen 0.46 exposure reads back as 0.53, `creator-lighting.ts:161-179`), and only `preview` goes through `storedCreatorLighting` (`workspace-state.ts:339`), so a multi-view workspace's lights node stops following refits after one save (`preview-view-graph.ts:112-113`) | Fixed (claude/lighting-setups) |
| PREV-136 | Low | Creator lighting (filter) | The 4×4 filter grid is too sparse for its radius (`shadow-filter.ts:14-18`, `creator-lighting.ts:338`): taps 2.3–4.5 texels apart with one-texel hardware filtering, so penumbrae likely staircase (hypothesis; needs a capture). Fix: spacing ≤ about 1 texel | Fixed (claude/lighting-setups) |

No drawn part uses it yet (the hair accessories read here are opaque meshes); needed before arm cyberware is drawn (render coverage rank 5). | PIPE-104 | Med | Character export | A cold export of a body texture mod's 8K maps (`base\4k\common\body\wa\textures\n02_naked.xbm`, `d02_naked.xbm`, `wa_base_rm02.xbm`, `fullbody_overlay_d01.xbm`: 8192²) runs WolvenKit's full-size PNG conversion, which peaked at 7.9 GB private in one `WolvenKit.CLI` (killed twice under an 8 GB guard), took 33 s, and then 25 s more on the host halving them to the served 4096 (`scaleTextures`). Proposal: decode `.xbm` natively and serve the largest mip within `SERVED_TEXTURE_MAX` (an 8K texture carries its 4K mip), so neither WolvenKit nor the halving runs | Fixed in claude/native-textures (see below) |
| PIPE-105 | Low | Clothing | A clothed V's first preparation reads every item factory (`factoryTable`: the game's six plus every `.xl` factory, 642 on the reference route) through WolvenKit: 35 s and 10 launches. The native reader refuses every one (`C2dArray has N bytes of data after its properties that this reader does not decode`: the `compiledHeaders`/`compiledData` tail). Later preparations read them from WolvenKit's JSON cache in 0.4 s. Proposal: decode `C2dArray`'s compiled tail natively, verified with `tools/native-cr2w-diff.ts` on the cached factories, then add it to `NATIVE_ROOTS` | Open |
| PIPE-106 | High | Character export | A body part a mod overrides could be drawn from the base game's copy. WolvenKit 9.0.1 can't export the KS UV texture framework's left arm (`a0_000_pwa_base_hq__l.mesh`: its garment flags hold two bytes per vertex where WolvenKit reads four, "Unable to read beyond the end of the stream" in `WriteGarmentParametersToMesh`); the exporter then retried with the game folder, where WolvenKit silently exports the base game's arm instead (raw `441ced0b…`, not the framework's `a585d5bc…`), and cached it under the framework's key. The vanilla arm's UVs sampled the framework's full-body atlas (`d02_naked.xbm`), so the forearm showed the atlas's feet and their dark padding: a foot and red patches on the left forearm. The game draws the framework's arm, whose UVs sit in the atlas, in both censorship modes. Fix: a retry with the game folder is checked against the raw the source itself gave; a mismatch is discarded and the source's own copy goes to the repair route, which now also empties short garment flags (`mesh-export-repair-2`) and exports the copy without the game folder unless materials are asked for, refusing any uncooked raw that isn't the packed copy; entries written with the game folder before the check are exported again for the character details (`rawChecked`). Tests: `tests/export-substitution.test.ts` | Fixed in claude/body-uv-uncensored |

Architecture half done in claude/platform-step7 (26 Sep): the scene host in `platform/scene/` draws the V through its detail loader and per-template adapters, features draw through `FeatureRenderer`s given only the scene port, and the rendering-boundary test covers the host and eye makeup's renderer |

| CORE-01 | High | Core | Autosave loop: save status re-triggers persist every ~180 ms with no edits | **Fixed** (claude/cleanup-core, 25 Sep) |

| CORE-03 | High | Core (design) | Presets/Undo/routing only understand eye-makeup recipes; needs domain registry + general preset model before CC controls | Partly fixed: [feature-module platform](feature-module-platform.md) step 1 (claude/platform-step1, 26 Sep) routes every action through one registry of system families and the eye-makeup module, with no fallback; step 2 (claude/platform-step2, 26 Sep) puts presets, the library and the workspace on looks with per-feature parts and editor memory, and eye makeup's actions are pure `capability`/`apply` in the registry; step 3 (claude/platform-step3) versions each layer model. Step 4 (claude/platform-step4) gives each look one Undo history. Step 5 (claude/platform-step5, 26 Sep, without its file moves) adds the presentation's feature facades, a live document per feature with generic feature handlers and `app.transaction`, view contributions for panels, layouts and activity sources, and per-look locking of newer data. The step-5 file moves (claude/platform-step5-moves, 26 Sep) put the layered-makeup engine in `engines/layered-makeup/`, eye makeup's view in `features/eye-makeup/view/` and the view list in `compose/`, with boundary tests for engines and feature views. The step-5 moves review's fixes (claude/cleanup-engine, 26 Sep) give the engine no region of its own (a feature hands it a `LayeredMakeupRegion`), feature views a narrow `FeatureViewContext`, and the feature core an allowlist with transitive checks. Open: steps 8–9 and step 7's follow-up (eye makeup's preview device, raster client and editors; the exporter and verifier; the eye-makeup modules that application, host and package code still import stay at the top of `src/` until then, each with its destination, blocking importer and step in the [design's table](feature-module-platform.md#file-moves)), and the shell couplings recorded in the [boundary assessment](ui-architecture-boundary.md#open-work) |

CORE-09 made worse | **Fixed** (claude/cleanup-core2, 25 Sep) |
| PREV-01 | High | Preview export | Incomplete WolvenKit exports cached as complete; preview permanently stuck until the game changes | **Fixed** (claude/wolvenkit-fetch, 25 Sep) |

| PREV-03 | Med | Preview export (design) | Material chains resolved by WolvenKit's view of the game folder, not the resolver's winning archives. Worse since PIPE-01: Build cuts the plate from the head the launch route loads, while the preview still reads only `archive/pc/content` (`preview-core-recipe.ts:32` comment claims they share one head) | Mostly fixed. Brows, lashes and hair follow the launch route's winning archives (claude/render-resolver). The head's geometry and skin chain do too (claude/skin-material): the character record carries the resolved head and its `skin.mt` chunk, and the renderer draws the skin on the core head when the two are the same surface, otherwise the resolved head itself. The eyes do too (claude/eye-render): the record's `eyes` slot carries the resolved eye component, and the base-game eye remains only as the fallback for layered designs. Open: the eye plate is still cut from the base-game head in the preview (Build cuts it from the launch route's) |

**Low findings** (picked up opportunistically). Paths are relative to `projects/xf-studio/authoring`.

- **PREV-09..16** (preview export, 25 Sep focused review): silent fallback to unhashed prepared files; host precedence drift; non-atomic cache write; no cache eviction or work-folder sweep; duplicate fingerprints; synchronous hashing on the request path; readiness passed via data attributes; test gaps (loader, error mapping, cache reuse).

- **PIPE-15:** a candidate that fails the second result gate stays in `package-candidates/` (`desktop/build.ts:206-212`).
- **PIPE-16:** Fixed (plate inputs hashed at start and end; morph count comes from the plate recipe).
- **PIPE-17:** `reg.exe` output is decoded as UTF-8, and process output is decoded per chunk (multi-byte splits).
- **PIPE-18:** error codes are mapped by matching message prefixes; needs a typed `PackageFilterError`.
- **PIPE-19:** stale comments in `mod-branding.ts` and `eye-plate-cache.ts` (the publish race comment).
- **PIPE-20:** plate derivation parses large JSON on the host event loop; move it to a worker.
- **PIPE-21:** selector indices shift when presets are omitted; save behaviour unproven. Add to the in-game test card.
- **PIPE-22:** the game-folder ArchiveXL bundle is listed non-recursively and outside source discovery.
- **UI-13:** Fixed in alpha readiness (plain library chip and autosave wording).
- **UI-14:** Fixed in claude/ui-polish (the palette asks each command's availability once per opening; running one asks again).
- **UI-15:** Fixed in claude/ui-polish (theme and preference changes go through their capability and say a refusal; every camera and light change reports through `rt.report`).
- **UI-16:** Fixed in alpha readiness (CSP, navigation guard, 415, identifiers renamed).
- **UI-17:** `desktop/update-electrobun.ts` is dead and unchecked; two TypeScript versions (5.9.3 vs 7.0.2).
- **UI-18:** `about.css` and boot-watchdog styles aren't tokenised; duplicate font-size tokens; palette input has `outline:none`.
- **CORE-12:** a fresh workspace opens the oldest collection and adds an "Unsaved preset".
- **CORE-13:** dead history helpers; glitter measurements never pruned and keyed by layer IDs that repeat across presets; wrong import consequence text.
- **CORE-14:** confusing names (`selectedCollection` returns a preset ID); `ReadonlyDeep` defined three times; shared mutable `glitterChoices`; stale test counts in the boundary doc.
- **CORE-15:** preview reports ready for one frame after an edit; geometry cache recopies on double updates. The reason-code part (codes matched from message text) is fixed in claude/platform-step1.
- **UI-19:** Fixed in claude/alpha-polish (the category is a plain label while Eye makeup is the only one).
- **UI-20:** Fixed in claude/alpha-polish (see below).
- **PIPE-26:** Fixed in claude/cleanup-pipeline2 (plain `plate_source_incomplete` stop).
- **PIPE-27:** Fixed in claude/cleanup-pipeline2 (typed `eyePlateHead` setting in Game & tools).
- **PIPE-07/PIPE-17 (extended):** the verifier's WolvenKit calls and the desktop Bun probe were moved onto the shared runners in claude/cleanup-pipeline2; `dotnet-runtime.ts` still adds another UTF-8-decoded `reg.exe` reader duplicating `install-detection-host.ts`.
- **PREV-17:** the WolvenKit post-install probe ignores the cancel signal (`wolvenkit-setup-host.ts:314`); Cancel does nothing for up to 30 s.
- **PREV-18:** WolvenKit unpacking runs synchronously on the server, and the .NET registry check re-runs every 3 s while the card polls every 500 ms.
- **PREV-19:** test gaps: `tool-download.ts` stall/redirect/length/oversize paths, `createInstalledHeadSource` end to end (its completeness gate is now unit-tested) and `process-tree`. The PIPE-23 and PIPE-24 cases are covered.
- **CORE-21:** Fixed (claude/cleanup-core2): shift edits are labelled "Shift colour" or "Shift strength".
- **CORE-22:** Fixed in claude/cleanup-pipeline2 (dead eligibility exports removed).
- **CORE-23:** Fixed (claude/cleanup-core2): one `historyTimeline()` mapper in `authoring-history.ts` serves both.
- **CORE-24:** Fixed (claude/cleanup-core2): the first collection keeps every editor-memory field except the recipe.
- **CORE-08/CORE-15 (extended):** new actions were added to both the descriptor table and the hand-kept `recipeKinds`/`undoPolicy()` sets (fixed in claude/cleanup-core2, see CORE-08); new refusals came back as `invalid_value` instead of `incompatible_mode` (fixed in claude/platform-step1: a refusal states its code).

Now the lock records the runner's process start time, a stale lock is moved aside and checked before it is removed, the refusal names the lock file, and `bridge.kill` falls back to the `KILL` file when the pipe is busy (tests in `tools/test/mcp.test.ts`).

- **NATIVE-44** (second native reader pass, review at `36b5c87`), Open:
  - **NATIVE-44:** a native answer's host-side copy and re-serialisation sit outside the worker's budgets (a crafted resource could stall the host and spike its heap), and the decode queue is FIFO with no cancellation, so a stopped prefetch still runs ahead of a person's change (`native-decode.ts`, `resource-graph.ts`). Partly eased: a request may now ask for `background` priority (the catalogue's texts do), which waits until no other request does; resolver and prefetch reads still share one FIFO.

- **NATIVE-54..57, PIPE-107..109** (native textures and arm fix review at `15941da`/`26d0dea`), Open:
  - **NATIVE-54:** textures larger than 8192² with no smaller mip pass the checks, decode fully, then fail PNG encoding as `internal` and fall back to a full-size WolvenKit export; refuse early with a budget error or box-halve to the cap (reproduced).
  - **NATIVE-55:** raw and deprecated formats have no oracle evidence; padded raw rows would pass the size check and show sheared.
  - **NATIVE-56:** BC7 allocates three arrays per block and `hasAlpha()` decodes mode 4–7 blocks twice.
  - **NATIVE-57:** the texture oracle tool holds every decoded mip 0 in memory at once.
  - **PIPE-107:** with the texture reader off, textures still leave the geometry launch and cost a second WolvenKit launch.
  - **PIPE-108:** a failed WolvenKit fallback launch for refused textures marks the whole archive failed, dropping its geometry and native textures (`native-texture-export.ts:197`, `character-detail-service.ts:614`).
  - **PIPE-109:** the substitution check trusts an answer whose no-game copy's file is missing, the repair's with-game attempt skips the check without a `.mesh`, `collect` gets the game-folder flag per request rather than per launch (latent), and old cache entries without `rawChecked` are trusted.

- **UI-106, CORE-94..98 and three unnumbered Lows** (view graph review at `635b0d7`), **Fixed** (claude/cleanup-view-graph, 27 Sep; see below):
  - **UI-106:** `view.setTool` doesn't check head readiness; a toggle made before the head loads isn't saved.
  - **CORE-94:** Undo/Redo replays a views array whose nodes an unrecorded structure edit removed: `state()` throws and a reload loses all views (reproduced; High once P4 adds real structure edits).
  - **CORE-95:** history coalescing is time-only and `seal()` is never called: two drags within 1 s merge, a paused drag splits (reproduced).

- **PREV-126** (review at `9f71a56`), Open:
  - **PREV-126:** the part pool counts parts and texels, not geometry or bone textures; eviction can dispose geometry a pending load shares.
- **UI-108** (review at `9f71a56`), **Fixed** (claude/expressions-drawer, 27 Sep):
  - **UI-108:** the expression drawer is composed only from the library (`features/expressions/view/drawer.ts`): GroupSection per region (the `details` whose `summary` held a button is gone), PairControl and SliderWithValue per control, SearchField over the controls and over Start from, a TreeView for Start from, value and confirm popovers for save, rename and delete. The ratchet's allowance for the file is removed (0 ad hoc controls). The library gained `openConfirmPopover` and TreeView's `onMenu` (right-click, Shift+F10, the Menu key through the `rows.menu` binding), and TreeView now moves its roving tab stop with focus that arrives by any route. Tests: `ui-component-ratchet`, the TreeView context-menu test in `ui-components`, `expressions-feature` (start-from links), `facial-host` (the inert check), `facial-preview`, `expression-samples`.
- **PREV-120, PREV-125, CORE-99, CORE-100, CORE-102 and the Lows PREV-121..124, CORE-101, CORE-103..107** (review at `9f71a56`), **Fixed** (claude/cleanup-host-facial, 27 Sep):
  - **PREV-120:** a batch prepared ahead reads through a stoppable view of the graph (`resource-graph.ts` `stoppableGraph`). Once it is stopped, reads it hasn't started reject and reads that finish answer the stop, so it goes no further down its chains; reads WolvenKit already has finish and stay memoised for everyone, and no memoised model holds the stop. The clothes step passes the stop on instead of logging it as a failure. The prefetcher waits for a stopped batch (its own or a replaced job's) to settle before the next one (`PrefetchDeps.settled`, the host's `warming` set), a stopped batch no longer grows the next one, and time spent waiting for a person's change or a stopped batch isn't counted toward the 10-minute limit. Tests: the view over a fixture graph, repeated pre-emption with one batch in flight, a replaced job waiting.
  - **PREV-125:** every file-system call of the prepared-files checks takes one of 16 slots shared by all walks at once (`prepared-files.ts` `limited`; a finished call hands its slot on), per-entry work runs in bounded chunks, and eviction first only counts the size, listing entries only when over budget. Test: three concurrent checks never exceed 16 calls in flight.
  - **CORE-99:** a solver program that can't be started is "not set up" in plain words (`SOLVER_NOT_SET_UP`), and the installed expressions still load. Tests: a throwing spawner, and the real spawner with no such program.
  - **CORE-100:** a solve has a 15 s timeout (`SOLVE_TIMEOUT_MS`): the stuck process is stopped and the next solve starts a new one. A line that isn't an answer is logged and fails the oldest waiting request (the host sends one at a time); the stream decoders keep split UTF-8 sequences. Tests: the real line protocol over a process's pipes (Bun runs a stand-in program: split and joined lines, a printed warning, a lost answer, a crash mid-request) and the timeout.
  - **CORE-101:** restarts are budgeted per 10 minutes (3), so the budget refills; `state()` restarts a stopped solver or says plainly that it stopped, never ready for an exited process, and returns a copy of the solver state; the drawer's Try again asks the host for its state first. Test: crash, restart, budget spent, refill.
  - **CORE-102:** the facial JSON cache is kept within 768 MB (least recently used first, the face's own files kept), start points for the 8 newest installations; temporary extraction folders over an hour old are swept once per session; "Clear prepared game files" counts and clears the facial cache too (`CharacterDetailHost.attachPrepared`, both hosts) and forgets the preparation. Test.
  - **CORE-103:** start points are cached by the winning archives' identities (face, table, photo-mode face rig and its patches), and the cache records the animation sets' archives and is read again when one changed. Test: a mod providing the set with a changed clip.
  - **CORE-104:** each page's solves carry a page id (`client`): a newer pose replaces only the same page's waiting one, and pages take turns (at most 8 waiting). Test.
  - **CORE-105:** a solver checkout without git history (the tools-folder location a consented download fills) is accepted when every module of the facial and `bartmoss` packages matches the reviewed pin's content (`bake_idle_face.py` `PIN_MODULES`, line endings normalised); an extra or changed module is refused.
  - **CORE-106:** the solve and part-preset endpoints read their bodies within their limits (`readBodyText`: a declared length over the limit is refused first) and refuse JSON that isn't an object with 400. Tests.
  - **CORE-107:** a masculine V's head says his live expressions come later (`FACE_MASCULINE`) instead of reporting a skeleton mismatch. Test.
  - **PREV-121:** an evaluation's work is bounded (`DEFORMATION_LIMITS.work`, 65,536 units: joints, extended transforms, sources, outputs and channels): a program at the other bounds is refused, and one at the work bound evaluates in milliseconds. **PREV-122:** every number is bounded (|x| ≤ 10⁶), and a degenerate pose keeps the pose it had before the program wrote it, so no NaN reaches the bones. **PREV-123:** the compiler returns its program through the parser, so it emits only what the parser reads (node notes capped at 64). **PREV-124:** the parser enforces the order: an added transform's parent comes before it, `extend` defines added transforms in order, and an op names only transforms already defined. Tests.

- **SAVE-05..09, NATIVE-60..63** (review at `9f71a56`), **Fixed** (claude/cleanup-save-mesh; see below):
  - **SAVE-05:** the expanded buffer (up to 256 MB) is allocated from declared sizes before any chunk is checked. **SAVE-06:** the world-object walk is one synchronous 649 ms step on the main thread (reproduced). **SAVE-07:** a junctioned Saved Games folder reads as not found (reproduced). **SAVE-08:** "Open a save file" reads the whole file before the 128 MB check. **SAVE-09:** no hostile-input or mutation tests for the save engines.
  - **NATIVE-60:** `has()` ignores the morph's `baseKey` while `cached()` checks it. **NATIVE-61:** mesh (and texture) cache identity omits the resource reader's version and type-data hash. **NATIVE-62:** the joint count can exceed `maxBones`. **NATIVE-63:** a zero-vertex LOD 1 chunk writes invalid glTF.
- **SAVE-10** (found in claude/cleanup-save-mesh), Open:
  - **SAVE-10 (Low):** a package's status check is still one step: a 2.31 save's `StatsSystem` is a single object of about 24 MB, which decodes in about 0.5 s on the main thread (the world-object walk now yields in steps of at most about 40 ms). Fix: decode packages in a worker, or make `decodeChunk` resumable.

## New subsystems since last review

- **Remembered view state** (claude/ui-state-persistence, UI-133): `src/studio-ui/view-state.ts` (the bound store: session memory until the shell binds the preference-backed one at mount; `RememberedSet`) and `src/studio-ui/scroll-anchor.ts` (anchor capture and restore over `data-view-key`, `ScrollMemory` with MutationObserver/ResizeObserver retries, `flushScrollMemories` on pagehide in the capture phase). A deep review should look at:
  - the module-level bound store (one per page; two instances of a panel share their folds and scroll keys);
  - the capture-phase ordering assumption: scroll anchors are dispatched before the workspace save's own `pagehide`/`visibilitychange` flush;
  - restore patience (30 s) against a slow first catalogue, and programmatic scrolls during a pending restore (the Character list's reveal of the V's choice is overridden by the next retry);
  - the cost of `getBoundingClientRect` over every keyed element at each debounced record (Character panel: a few hundred).

- **Choice previews** (claude/choice-previews, [choice previews design §10](../character-customization/choice-previews-design.md#phase-1-status-hairstyles)): `src/choice-preview.ts` (pure: kinds from the preview detail, sources from a record's detail slot with coverage from the template table's new `coverage` field, content keys, the colour matrix and its script twin, the row camera), `src/choice-preview-render.ts` (raw WebGL 2 on an OffscreenCanvas: ordered-threshold coverage at 2× with 4× MSAA, resolve and 2:1 blit, readback, WebP), `src/choice-preview-worker.ts` (a new browser entry, also in the desktop bundle and canary list), `src/choice-preview-service.ts` (the page's priority queue), `src/choice-preview-host.ts` and `src/choice-preview-server.ts` (store, source index, endpoint), `CharacterDetailHost.previewSources`/`derivePreview` (a third preparation lane beside a person's change and preparing ahead), `studio-ui/components/choice-preview.ts` and the `choiceSize.set` UI preference. Review focus:
  - the host's lanes: a derivation never beside a person's change or a batch (`previewing` in `request()` and `foregroundIdle`, `PREVIEW_HOLD_MS` holding the next batch back while the page asks), the threshold rule (a derivation past its writing step is waited for, not stopped), and a derivation that waits behind a long batch (capped at 10 minutes);
  - `prepareCharacterDetails` without `manifests` and with `skipDangles` (the choice's own manifest stays the authority; the source is kept under the stamp it was derived from);
  - the index lookup for every shown choice before preparing ahead re-checks it (a stale picture of the same choice is shown until replaced, design Q9), and what replaces it after a mod update in the same session (nothing yet: a reload);
  - the worker's memory per job (sources up to 44 MB fetched whole, every GL object disposed per job) and context-loss recreation once;
  - the measurement hook `globalThis.xfsChoicePreviews` (debug and stats only) and the `?background` flag on character file reads (skips `noteAsk`).

- **Lighting setups** (claude/lighting-setups, [creator lighting §9](../../knowledge/creator-lighting.md#lighting-setups)): `src/lighting-setups.ts` (pure: complete setups, built-ins as templates, forking, own setups, the light editor's operations, validation and the migration of workspaces saved before) and `src/lighting-setup-stage.ts` (the one Three path for every setup, replacing `lighting-preset-stage.ts` and `studio-light-rig.ts`). Review focus:
  - the lights node codec's two forms (new and legacy) and the legacy mirror recomputed on every edit (`preview-view-graph.ts`);
  - forking inside one View and lighting step, and coalescing a drag that starts on a built-in;
  - the light-list adapter's in-place updates and shadow configuration against the old rigs (parity tests in `tests/lighting-setups.test.ts`);
  - `PreviewPort.setLighting` as the device's single lighting input.
- **Swatch contrast and the swatch card** (claude/colour-swatches, [choice previews design §5.5](../character-customization/choice-previews-design.md#55-swatches)): `src/swatch-contrast.ts` (pure OKLab per-set enhancement: per-axis gain curves of the set's spread, overall-separation weight, hue cap, gamut clamp), `src/cc-swatch-display.ts` (per maker group sets, `truth` kept, memoised in `CharacterContextActions.swatches`), library component `studio-ui/components/swatch-card.ts` (card and contrast marker) wired in the Character panel by delegation; swatch rules v2 in `cc-swatch.ts` (decal diffuse at 512 px, coverage² for brows).
- **Saved layouts** (claude/saved-layouts, [view graph design §4.5](view-graph-design.md#45-saved-layouts-option-c-first-step)): `src/layout-library.ts` (pure: the `xfs/layout-library-1` library in `UIPreferences.layouts`, eleven `layouts.*` actions with capabilities and bounds, the kept working state, the automatic size switch), `UIPreferenceActions` delegating to it, `studio-ui/layouts.ts` (the Layouts menu, palette commands and size-class switching over a narrow host), `DockView.load` (a whole arrangement at once), and in `dock/persist.ts` `sameDockState` plus two restore changes that apply at every start too: panels the catalogue doesn't know are kept parked with their places instead of dropped, and a panel the saved tree parked whose module is shown now returns to its parked place. The value popover gained option switches (`ValueOption`). A new persisted schema and a change to how every saved layout restores, so it should be in the next deep review. Review focus: the restore changes against older saved layouts, the "changed" comparison's cost per paint (cached by state key), the library's growth inside the workspace budget (24 layouts, 256 KB), a switch while a gesture or form edit is open (`finishInput`), and automatic switching racing a manual one on resize.
- **Skin scatter and the input-variant swap** (claude/sss-port, [skin reference §11](../materials/shader-skin.md#11-screen-space-scatter-in-the-preview-threejs)): `src/platform/scene/pass-variants.ts` (the swap: variants are forward materials seen through a prototype, with their own defines, blend state, program key and event listeners; roles declared through `declarePass` in `platform/api/scene.ts`, undeclared surfaces occlude or are left out by rule; variants freed with their forward material), `src/platform/scene/skin-scatter.ts` (S0–S2 three-target input, P0/P1, the two passes and combine, scissor, slots, wrap gating, target release), `src/platform/scene/skin-scatter-kernel.ts` (pure: the game's kernel builder and table, slot assignment, a CPU reference blur); scatter-input branches under `XFS_SCATTER_INPUT` in `skin-material.ts`, `face-decal-material.ts`, `brow-material.ts`, `plate-blend.ts` and `mouth-occlusion.ts`; the delta add in `linear-display.ts`; the orchestration in `lighting-preset-stage.ts`. Review focus:
  - the prototype variant: which properties Three writes on a material during render (they must land on the variant, not the forward material), `id`/`uuid`/`version`/`_listeners` shadowing, and staleness when a forward material changes without `needsUpdate`;
  - the swap's restore on every path (throw mid-draw, material arrays, hidden lines and points) and its per-frame traversal cost with many meshes;
  - blend state per attachment: one `SrcAlpha/OneMinusSrcAlpha`, `Zero/One` state for all three targets, each output's alpha as its weight, and the class and slot never changed by a decal;
  - the shader contract names (`xfsScatterOut1/2`, `xfsScatterE`, `xfsScatterEUnder`) across five material patches and the occluder patch's `alphatest_fragment` hook and early `return`;
  - target lifetime (created lazily, released when nothing scatters, resized with the drawing buffer) and the scissor's bind-pose bounds;
  - `render` restoring the renderer's target, clear colour, `autoClear` and the scene background.

- **Pose playback and the Poses module** (claude/pose-panel, [pose library design](../animation/pose-library-design.md) P2–P3): `src/pose-clip.ts` (a sampled pose as a body clip in glTF axes with the record's placement; moving poses loop) and `src/pose-sample.ts`; `IdleAnimation` gains a pose mode, a face override and joint bounds; `FaceDriver.setLent`/`deltas` and `composePreviewMotion`'s `poseChanged` (a held expression composes over a posed head); `SceneHost.setPose`, posed framing (`posedBodyFrame`, `posedBodySubject`) and posed clip planes; `MotionActions` holds a pose as the body source (`holdPose`, supersession, rollback, the stored pose as view state `preview.pose`); the host's moving-clip frames (`clipMotion`); garment visual tags in the character record (`garment.tags`) and `CharacterDetailStatus.garmentTags`; the module `src/features/poses/` (tree, preferences, service, facade, and a panel composed from the component library's SearchField, TreeView and favouriteToggle) with **a new per-user host document** (`features/poses/host/preferences-store.ts`, `pose-preferences.json` beside the settings, `/api/pose-preferences` and `/api/verification/pose-preferences` on both hosts). A new subsystem and a new host write path, so a deep review is due. Review focus: the preferences endpoint (origin and size checks, revision guard, the verification copy never writing the person's file), the motion service's supersession and rollback when poses, Still and idles race, the face driver's lend and take-back (the blink muted, no double writes), the posed bounds and clip planes for placed poses (swim offsets), the moving-clip payload bound (`MOTION_FRAMES`), the outfit filter's tags from records that predate them, and the tree rows' composite IDs (a pose in Favourites, Recent and its category at once).
- **Hair physics** (claude/hair-physics, [hair physics plan](../animation/hair-physics-plan.md) P0–P3): `src/dangle-spec.ts` (pure: a dangle component's `.rig` and `.animgraph` into a bounded spec, class defaults filled, anything the executable read doesn't cover left rigid with a plain note; `parseDangleSpec` for the browser), `src/dangle-solver.ts` (pure: the game's Dyng simulation from [knowledge §5](../../knowledge/hair-physics.md#5-solver-arithmetic-231-executable), `Float64Array` state, allocation-free frames, the Studio's settle rule), `src/dangle-motion.ts` (Three adapter: V's joint deltas into the solver's model space, chain joints out to virtual drivers), `src/dangle-host.ts` (host: rig and graph by archive precedence, specs stored content-addressed beside the records). The resolver keeps component bindings (`ComponentModel.skinning`, `.animated`); the plan finds each mesh's dangle component (`dangleOf`); the record gains an optional per-component `dangle`; `IdleAnimation` binds chain joints to their part's drivers and runs the simulation on game frames of the motion clock (`setDangles`, `setPhysics`); the scene node gains `physics` and the motion family `motion.setPhysics` (Motion panel switch).

- **Component library** (claude/ui-components; [component library](ui-component-library.md)): `src/studio-ui/components/`, the one entry point for every control panels and features compose. It holds:
  - the tab strip and panel header behind the dock;
  - `iconButton`, `SliderWithValue`, `PairControl`, `GroupSection`, `SearchField`, `Combobox`, `TreeView` / `favouriteToggle`, `FolderSetting` and `progressBar`;
  - the layout primitives (`stack`, `blockSection`, `PageHeader`, `propertyList`, `codeBlock`, `SplitView`);
  - the catalogue and live specimens in `style-guide/library.ts` and `library-demo.ts`.

  It comes with the Component-first rule's ratchet (`tests/ui-component-ratchet.test.ts`) and the dock's summon rule (`summonPanel`, `lastPlace` in the saved layout). Review focus:
  - the dock's `lastPlace` parse and restore (`restorePlace`, shared with parking), and floating placement when there is no home;
  - `HeaderFitter`'s one ResizeObserver: fit loops, and cost with many groups;
  - `TreeView` windowing: focus kept across updates, the flat ARIA structure, trailing actions out of the tab order;
  - `Combobox` focus and blur handling;
  - the ratchet's scan (what it counts, and what a string or comment hides).
- **Mottle, vector extensions V4** (claude/mottle, [vector engine extensions §7](vector-engine-extensions.md#7-mottle-skin-like-breakup-for-eye-makeup)). Not a new module family: a new stage inside the layered-makeup engine's shared coverage evaluator and a new layer-model slot. `src/engines/layered-makeup/mottle.ts` (the `mottle-1` model: validation, presets, seeds, the seeded tileable noise tile with its mip chain and two small LRU caches, and `prepareMottle`); `effects.mottle` validated as model slot `mottle` in `layer-models.ts` (an unknown effect or mottle model is a newer build's); `recipe.ts` applies it after the coverage of each sample in `raster`, `rasterWindow`, `createRasterJob` (the mirrored pixel pairing kept, streaks included) and the scalar `coverage` oracle, and needs the region's new `skin` scale (`LayeredMakeupRegion.skin`, carried to the worker in `RasterRegion`); `xfs/recipe-12` holds it (`recipe-schema.ts`); four actions `effect.mottle.enable`/`set`/`shuffle`/`preset` and the facade's `mottleCatalogue()`; the Mottle section of the Pigment & edge inspector. Review focus: that unmottled rasters stay byte-identical and as fast (frozen hashes, the reference timing in [raster performance](raster-performance.md#mottle)); the paired raster's opposite-pixel side rule against the scalar oracle; tile determinism across engines (integer hashing and + − × ÷ √ in the tile; `Math.log2`, `cos` and `sin` only per raster); cache growth (4 seeds, 8 mixtures); the reach test using unmottled coverage (`layerCoverageSampler`); and whether the verifier's mapping gate stays comfortable for strongly mottled looks (the window map and the 4096 head reference sample the tile at nearly the same level).
- **Expressions, feature module #2, and the live facial preview** (claude/expressions-p1, [expression editor design](../animation/expression-editor-design.md#phase-1-status) phase 1). New pure engine `src/engines/facial-rig/` (vocabulary, vectors, a reader of animation buffers' float keys, solved poses in glTF axes); the feature `src/features/expressions/` (part `xfs/expression-part-1`, six actions, the Studio module, the drawer view); host `src/facial-host.ts` and `src/facial-catalogue.ts` (the face rig, setup and blink from the winning archives, WolvenKit `unbundle --hash` then `convert s` into `facial/json/`, the installed photo-mode expressions from the winning table and the photo-mode face rig's sets, ArchiveXL patches included) with `/api/facial`, `/api/facial/expressions` and `/api/facial/solve` on both hosts; **a new external process**: `tools/facial_solver_server.py`, the pinned IO Suite solver kept warm and fed JSON lines; client `src/facial-preview.ts` (newest-wins solves, readiness) and `src/browser-facial-device.ts`; the scene's `src/platform/scene/face-driver.ts` with a third owner in `preview-motion.ts` and `GameBlink.setMuted`; generic feature form-control transactions in `StudioApplication` (`featureControlBegin/Edit/Commit/Cancel`); sparse live-feature exports (`LiveFeatures.export`, `liveFeatureStates`, `withLiveFeatures`); and **part presets in the library** (`src/part-preset-store.ts`: a new `part_presets` table beside the released ones, `/api/part-presets`; `src/part-presets.ts` and the async `presets` family). A new subsystem, a new host process and a new library table, so a deep review is due. Review focus: the solver process's lifetime (spawn, a crash mid-request, orphaning when the host dies: it exits on stdin EOF), the host's single-flight queue and `superseded` answers, request bounds (64 KB body, 64 frames, vector validation), the extractor's hash matching of WolvenKit outputs and its temporary folders, the decoder's bounds on hostile buffers (`decodeClipTracks` against declared counts), the start-point cache key (installation fingerprint) going stale, `part_presets` forward migration and revision guards (alpha.1's list is tested), the face driver's head-bone check and restore, the sparse live-feature write-back (a part removed by Undo must leave the look), and the drawer's use of `prompt`/`confirm` for rename and delete.
- **Metal adapter and mouth occlusion** (claude/head-fixes): `src/metal-base-material.ts` (the `metal_base` template: parameters, a CPU surface reference and the Three program patch) with its `metal-base` entry in `render-templates.ts`; `src/mouth-occlusion.ts` (per-vertex light share of a part inside the head from its depth behind the drawn head, and a light-term patch any lit program takes), applied to the teeth slot by `materialAdapter`.
- **Native mesh reader** (claude/native-meshes, [native reader](../backlog/native-archive-reader.md#phase-4-meshes) phase 4): `src/native/mesh-blob.ts` (render-blob header, per-chunk vertex layouts, stream decoding), `morph-blob.ts` (morph target header, diffs and mapping), `mesh-glb.ts` (WolvenKit's GLB conventions, rig, sparse deltas), `mesh-decode.ts` (one mesh or morph target from an archive, the base mesh from the same archive), the worker's `geometry` message (`native-decode.ts`, `native-decode-serve.ts`), `src/native-geometry-export.ts` (native first per resource, WolvenKit fallback, cache under `xfs-native-mesh:<version>`) and `NativeDecoders` (the texture decoders' holder, generalised). **A native binary decoder over untrusted mod files.** Review focus: every stream, index and diff read bounded before allocation (`chunkLayout`'s stream checks against `vertexBufferSize`, the index range, `morphTargetLayout`'s diff and mapping ends), the output estimate before decoding (`maxOutputBytes`, `estimateBytes` at 150 bytes a vertex) and the delta budget, a hostile `numVertices`/`numIndices` pair against the render buffer, the joint-count rule from the rig (only when the names cover the skin), refusals staying typed (`unsupported`/`malformed`/`over-budget`), the base-mesh read's own session budget, the transferred buffers in the worker reply, and the geometry exporter's cache entries (`geometry.json` hashes trusted after `cache.read` verified the files).
- **Save Explorer** (claude/save-explorer, [save editor design](../save/save-editor-design.md) phase 1): **a binary reader over the person's own save files** and a new read-only host capability. Pure engines: `src/engines/save/` (the container codec: header, chunk table, LZ4, node table and tree, `SaveImage`) and `src/engines/red-object/` (FNV-1a hashes, the save's `TypeDatabase_v2`, the type oracle, the one package reader shared with `native/red-package.ts`, the `PersistencySystem2` walker, `final.redscripts` names). The feature `src/features/save-explorer/` (read model, service, facade, panel, host endpoints `GET /api/saves`, `/api/saves/file`, `/api/saves/types` on both hosts), `src/saves-host-sources.ts` (Saved Games from the registry or profile, the script bundle MO2 resolves), `src/browser-save-explorer-device.ts`, and the platform's part-less module seam (`ModuleService`, `port.module`, `moduleView`, `compose/module-services.ts`). `save-reader.ts` now reads through the container codec and the save's own type database; `saved-v.ts` holds the saved-V records the scene host reads. A deep review is due. Review focus: every bound on hostile saves (chunk and node counts, the tree builder's loops and depth, the package frame's variant detection accepting a non-package, the persistency walker's nesting and element counts, hex previews and inspector output caps), the saves endpoints' folder-name rule, link refusal and bounded reads, that no path, user name or other metadata field leaves the host, the names endpoint's size (170,000 names from a modded bundle), main-thread cost when opening a large save (1.6 s for a 7.8 MB end-game save, container expanded at once), and the module-service exception (actions outside the registry; [boundary](ui-architecture-boundary.md)).
- **Character panel taxonomy, expanders and help tips** (claude/panel-polish): `src/character-panel-sections.ts` now places every creator row into the panel's own sections by what it controls (`sectionOfRow`, `identifierWords`, `CharacterSectionHolds`; sections nest through `parent`), `src/studio-ui/expander.ts` (one disclosure for groups, sections, subsections, rows and author groups; `ExpandAll`), `src/studio-ui/help-tip.ts` (help icons and the page's one tip, replacing informational notes in every panel; controls' `help` and `section({ title, help })`), the `folded.set` UI preference, `PanelController.commands` (panel-owned palette commands) and the "Other mods" pool in `xfs/cc-panel-5` (`CcPanel.pools` deduplicated and `CcPanelOption.pool`, `pooledGroups`, `OWN_GROUP_MIN_CHOICES`; readers accept a host without them; the UI-59 size budget holds). Review focus: `sectionOfRow` on modded rows (a word that means two things, a slot whose first word is generic), the boundary test's widened vocabulary allowance for the taxonomy's words, the help tip's global listeners (Escape handled in capture only while a tip shows, click capture only on help icons), fold keys' bounds, and the section rebuild keyed by the nested tree's identity.
- **Native animation decoder and the pose catalogue** (claude/pose-readers, [pose library design](../animation/pose-library-design.md) P0–P1): `src/native/anim-set.ts` (pure: an `animAnimSet`'s clip index read record by record, the compressed key format written from the format, `animRig`'s bone table, sampling with slerp), `src/native/anim-decode.ts` (host adapter: index, clip and rig requests, a new request kind in `native-decode.ts`, `native-decode-serve.ts` and the worker), `src/tweakxl-yaml.ts` (a YAML reader that keeps tags and scalar text) folded into `src/tweakxl-overlay.ts` (TweakXL list operations: assignment, deletions, prepends, appends, `-once`, `-from`), `array:String` and `Vector3` in `src/tweakdb-flats.ts`, `animations:` in `src/archivexl-config.ts`, `src/pose-catalogue.ts` (pure domain), `src/pose-catalogue-host.ts` (host service with an on-disk set index), `src/pose-catalogue-server.ts` (`/api/poses` on both hosts), `src/pose-actions.ts` with `POSE_DESCRIPTORS`, and `tools/anim-oracle.ts`. **A native decoder of untrusted mod files and a new parser of every TweakXL file.** Review focus: counts and offsets in `decodeAnimClip` against the key block (every count is checked before allocation; the block may come from an inline `KARK` stream), the selective record walker's handling of struct records and handles past the export table, `parseTweakYaml` on hostile text (depth, node and alias limits, block-scalar indentation, flow collections spanning lines), the list rules' order against `TweakChangeset::Commit` (the `$base` chain of mutated lists is not modelled), the set-index cache shared by both body genders (pruned only past 20,000 entries), and the host holding decoded clips and rigs in memory without a byte cap.

- **Live-pose write path** (claude/bridge-batch4, `projects/xf-runtime-bridge`): the bridge's first reads and writes of engine memory outside script calls. `plugin/LivePoseMemory.cpp` finds a loaded animation set through RED4ext.SDK's `ResourceLoader::FindToken` (the loader and what releasing a token copy calls are resolved at load; missing ones switch the commands off), walks set → entry → animation → compressed buffer, checks every field offset it uses against the game's reflection data and the class sizes, and copies the constant keys; `core/LivePose.cpp` (pure, unit-tested) checks the five key runs against their counts and each other, the constant keys, the carrier contract, the rotation encoding, the keys hash and the write plan. `pose.live.apply` writes the XF carrier clip's constant keys in place (behind `allow_live_pose`, the -writes build only, with the carrier selected in photo mode), keeps the originals for undo and the kill switch and restores only into the same buffer. `photo.pose.set` (menu attributes 5 and 6, `writes::PoseSet`) and the carrier test package's build (`tools/live-pose/`, WolvenKit 9.0.1, a key-by-key verifier) come with it. A new kind of write (engine memory, not a script call), so a deep review is due before the first session uses it. Review focus: every read before the offset and span checks pass (none may dereference unchecked memory), the lifetime of the token copy and of the raw buffer pointer across the write (main thread only; a set unloaded between locate and write), the snapshot's identity check, the SharedPtr release path's relocated calls, torn reads by animation jobs (accepted risk, design §10), the carrier contract, and that the default and diagnostic packages can't write (`allow_live_pose` off, the carrier a separate package). [README](../../projects/xf-runtime-bridge/README.md), [design §3.2 and §4](../runtime/runtime-bridge-design.md#4-safety-model), [pose editor design §7.4](../animation/pose-editor-design.md#74-first-experiment-plan-one-supervised-session).
- **CET panel and re-arm** (claude/bridge-batch4): the first in-game XF interface (`cet/xf_runtime_bridge/panel.lua`, test packages only) and the plugin paths behind it: `XFBridge_Rearm` (a request carried out by `HandleRearm` on the Running tick) and `Bridge::Rearm` (join the old watcher, remove the `KILL` file, a new session identity, `GameThreadQueue::Reopen`, `Dispatcher::Revive`, restart the listener; `RestoreOnce::Reset` and `OptionsExchange::Reset`), and `XFBridge_PauseWrites` with the dispatcher's `writes_paused` gate. Re-arming undoes the kill switch's "until restart" guarantee, so a review should look at: every precondition (killed, the listener closed, the restore run) and what happens when one fails part-way (the bridge must stay killed), races between the pipe, watcher and game threads during the swap (the session fields rewritten while no bridge thread runs), a stale client or token reaching the new session, the pause gate's reach (every write, `photo.open` via `game.status`) and that it can't exceed `config.ini`, the in-process trust boundary (RB-48), and that CET actions run from `onUpdate`, never `onDraw`. [README](../../projects/xf-runtime-bridge/README.md), [design §4](../runtime/runtime-bridge-design.md#4-safety-model).
- **Deformation rig and preview idles** (claude/body-fidelity): `src/deformation-rig.ts` (pure: compiles a rig and its constraint-chain animation graph into a bounded program, evaluates it on a pose, and parses a served program back), `src/deformation-rig-host.ts` (the player entity's secondary animated components, compiled per record), the native reader's `animRig` appendix, the record's optional `rigs` (programs as content-addressed JSON beside the record), virtual drivers in `src/idle-animation.ts`; `src/idle-catalogue.ts` and `tools/prepare_body_idles.py` (the game's preview idles as local assets), `motion.setIdleClip`, `xfs/character-request-8` (`puppet: "creator"`, `bodyStateFor`), and the export skin route for morph targets (`MorphTargetSkin`). A new host capability (reading and compiling animation graphs) and a new request field, so a deep review is due. Review focus: the compiler's bounds and refusals on hostile graphs and rigs, the program parser, the appendix size check, the skin route's once-only note and its re-export of cached entries, the feet rule, and the idle catalogue's asset names. [Body animation](../../knowledge/body-animation.md).

- **View graph service** (claude/view-graph-p1, [view graph design](view-graph-design.md) P1): `src/platform/api/view-graph.ts` (types), `src/platform/core/view-graph.ts` (the DOM-free `ViewGraph`: views referencing scene, camera, lights, display and tools nodes; link, unlink, add and close view; reference-counted collection in the same commit; validation against slot codecs and scene-kind rules; `xfs/view-graph-1` parse and write; the session-only View and lighting history with coalescing; per-camera Back/Forward trails) and `src/preview-view-graph.ts` (the Studio's node codecs, the default one-view graph from the workspace's `preview`, the legacy mirror). `PreviewActions` now stores nothing and follows the graph for the view it draws; the workspace stores `views` only when the graph differs from the default. **The one owner of camera, light, display, scene and tool state, and a new persisted format.** Review focus: `parseViewGraph` on hostile or damaged `views` (bounds, ID reuse across slots, a view naming another slot's node), the main view's legacy-field overlay on read, the diff-based device applier in `PreviewActions` (order of port calls, a released head unsubscribing, `applied` changes never echoed), coalescing across Undo and structural steps, a camera jump recording the device's pose rather than the node's older one, and history steps restoring only the nodes they touched.
- **Module registry and derived view tools** (claude/view-graph-p1, P2): `src/platform/api/module.ts` (`StudioModule`, `ViewToolContribution`, derivation and completeness helpers), `src/platform/core/view-tools.ts` (the platform's tools), `src/compose/modules.ts` (the composition list), `src/features/eye-makeup/module.ts` (eye makeup's manifest, tools and summary), `src/view-actions.ts` (the `views` family: `view.setTool`, `view.undo`, `view.redo`), `StudioApplication.viewTools/viewSummaries/modules/views` and `port.views`; in studio-ui the derived toolbar, view menu, palette entries and Display toggles, the Modules menu, the Panels flyout grouped by module, run-time panel add and remove with parked layouts (`parkPanels`, `unparkPanels`, `parseTree`'s parked list) and collapsible groups. Review focus: the parked-place model (a whole group leaving and coming back beside its anchor at its share, floating and closed panels, places read before any panel leaves), the parser never re-adding a parked panel, `DockView.addPanels/removePanels` with visibility callbacks and a withdrawn panel's `reveal`, the collapse layout in row and column splits and floating windows, and module visibility staying out of the application (rule 6).
- **Maker groups for creator choices** (claude/character-panel-v2; cc-controls backlog 4a): `src/mod-makers.ts` (host: who made each mod, from Vortex's state through `inspectVortexSetup` within a 3 s budget when `Installation.vortexMods` is non-empty, hand-placed game-folder mods as one group), `VortexModIdentity.author`, `Installation.vortexMods`, `xfs/cc-panel-5` (`groups`, `modGroups`, `CcPanelOption.groups`, `isXfResource`, `choiceGroup`, `compareGroups`) and the grouped `ChoiceList` (folding headings, navigation across them). Review focus: Vortex's state read now also runs on every catalogue build of a Vortex-deployed game folder (budget and cache reuse with diagnostics' reads), author text from Vortex's state as untrusted display text (`makerText`: control characters out, 80 characters), the reader's bounds on `groups`/`modGroups`, and the list's roving tab stop when a folded group holds it.
- **Creator swatches, choice icons, the TweakXL overlay and the Character panel's hierarchy** (claude/character-panel-v2; cc-controls backlog Next 1, 3, 4): `src/cc-swatch.ts` (pure: the swatch plan per link family, the colour chunk and its colour, the compact text), `src/cc-swatch-host.ts` (background work on the catalogue's installation, one `resolveCharacter` per family's candidates, small native texture mips, a disk cache per family validated by the archives each read came from), `src/cc-icons.ts` and `src/cc-icon-host.ts` (atlas parts, sheets cut from the native texture reader's mip, kept by content key, served at `?sheet=&key=`), `src/tweakxl-overlay.ts` (a new reader of untrusted mod YAML: `r6/tweaks` files become a source kind in `source-discovery.ts` and `Installation.tweaks`), `xfs/cc-panel-4` (`CcSwatches`, `readSwatches`; now `xfs/cc-panel-5`), and `src/character-panel-sections.ts` (the derived hierarchy and contributions) rendered by `studio-ui/panels/character.ts`. The same branch makes Off the creator's own label (`labelledOff`, `OFF_TEXT_KEY`) instead of "adds nothing". A new host read path over mod files (YAML, atlases, texture mips) and a new served image endpoint, so a review should look at: the overlay's bounds (file size cap 8 MB, 100,000 instances, 512-character names, `$base` depth 16) and that Bun's YAML parser can't be made to allocate without bound; the sheet endpoint's key check and that it serves only sheets the host made; the swatch cache's validation (every read's winning archive and stamp) and concurrent writers (two hosts on one resolver cache); the family representative choice (a key a representative's chain can't answer tries two more members) and what "replaced" compares; the background work's yield and stop when a catalogue is rebuilt. [CC controls backlog](../backlog/cc-controls-and-presets.md#current-state), [choice icons design §7](../character-customization/choice-icons-design.md#7-as-built).
- **Native texture reader** (claude/native-textures, phase 3 of the native reader, PIPE-104): `src/native/bcn.ts` (BC1/3/4/5/7 block decoding, pure), `src/native/xbm-texture.ts` (the `rendRenderTextureBlobPC` layout, mip checks, the served mip, rows in WolvenKit's orientation, pure), `src/native/texture-decode.ts` (one texture from an archive to its PNG; a host adapter) and a `texture` message in the decode worker (`native-decode.ts`, `native-decode-serve.ts`: the PNG's buffer transferred); `encodePngRows` (a streamed PNG) in `src/png.ts`; `src/native-texture-export.ts` (`createNativeFirstExporter` over the WolvenKit exporter, its own cache identity, and `NativeTextureDecoders`, one worker per game folder), wired in `CharacterDetailHost.exporterFor`. **A native binary decoder over untrusted mod files** in every first preparation, so a deep review is due. Review focus: the layout checks before any pixel is read (mip sizes against placements and the slice, the data buffer's size against the header, sides and mip counts capped, pitches never trusted), the raised decoded-bytes budget for texture data (`TEXTURE_READ_LIMITS`, 160 MiB), BC7's bit reader on hostile blocks, the streamed encoder's backpressure and the worker's async texture path (a close message while a texture encodes), the fallback's per-texture accounting and its extra WolvenKit launch, the cache identity (never WolvenKit's) and `has` for the choice manifests, and the exporter only wrapping the host's own WolvenKit exporter. [Archive formats §10](../../knowledge/archive-format.md#10-textures-xbm), [native reader backlog](../backlog/native-archive-reader.md#phase-3-textures).
- **Teeth slot** (claude/head-completeness): the `teeth` detail slot end to end: `DETAIL_UI_SLOTS.teeth`, `teethLabel` and `oncePerPart` (first drawable component per name in an appearance, identical parts once) in `src/character-detail-plan.ts`; `xfs/render-detail-11` (`DETAIL_SLOTS` gains `teeth`); the loader never keeps the teeth as the skin under a decal (`keepSkin`); the renderer's visibility and draw order; `CharacterSlot` in `src/platform/api/scene.ts`; the preview panel's slot name; coverage now `rendered` for the teeth row. A new slot over existing adapters (skin and layered), not a new host capability. Review focus: the once-per-name rule's reach (teeth only), that no decal ever reads the teeth as its skin, and the scene-evidence `morphs`/`bones` for the teeth. The same branch ports the hair `.hp` bake and the hair environment light (`src/hair-colour-model.ts`, `src/hair-shading.ts`). [Head CC rendering](../../knowledge/head-cc-rendering.md#1-every-head-option-at-a-glance).
- **Native reader: creator texts and WolvenKit-optional routes** (claude/native-catalogue): the creator catalogue's on-screen texts (`JsonResource` holding `localizationPersistenceOnScreenEntries`) are read through the route's fetcher (`ResolverFetcher.fetchJsonResource`: natively first at `background` priority, gated per request on the payload class, `NativeDecodeRequest.payloads`; WolvenKit's JSON-resource mode otherwise), so a cold catalogue reads about 930 text resources natively and launches no WolvenKit. A route and the catalogue open without WolvenKit (`InstallationRoute.wolvenKitCli: null`; `characterRoute`), and only exports ask for it. The decode worker is released after 60 s idle and closes the game's Oodle library with its own `FreeLibrary` call through `bun:ffi`, because Bun's `close` doesn't unload a library on Windows (`oodle.ts` `moduleUnloader`). A new native call and a new host read path over mod files, so a review should look at the `FreeLibrary` pairing (one per `dlopen`, Bun's `close` never also called, no call into a closed library), the worker's close handshake, the payload gate, and the catalogue's text cache keyed by reader. Reviewed at `17d2585` (NATIVE-46..53, fixed in claude/cleanup-native-catalogue). [Native reader backlog](../backlog/native-archive-reader.md#integration), [archive formats §2](../../knowledge/archive-format.md#2-compression-kark-and-oodle).
- **Add to my mod manager** (claude/ui-polish, UI-82): `src/mod-install-host.ts` (the plan, consent by token, the MO2 mod-list row placed by `mo2-placement.ts`, the running-MO2 wait, plain refusals; over the existing trusted `mod-install-transport.ts`), `/api/mod-install` on both hosts, `src/mod-install-actions.ts` (the port's `modInstall`) and the review sheet (`studio-ui/panels/mod-install-sheet.ts`). The first host capability that writes into the person's own MO2 profile and game folder outside a diagnostic stage, so a deep review is due. Review focus: the token and what it covers, the modlist write (checked against the planned text, the previous copy kept, written beside and moved), the refusal of foreign folders and files, duplicates elsewhere, the running-MO2 check, and that no framework or other row moves. Reviewed at `536bff3` (INSTALL-01..12, UI-98..100); receipts are now per user on this computer and interrupted installs recover on the next plan (claude/cleanup-install).
- **Production native reader** (claude/native-integration): the resolver reads every resource natively first and WolvenKit per resource. `ResolverFetcher`, `NativeAnswerFiles`, `openNativeRoute` and `nativeRouteStamp` in `src/resolver-host.ts` (the resolver host joins the clothing host as the only importers of `src/native/`); one decoder per game folder in `src/installation-registry.ts` (opened asynchronously before a route's first open, shared by routes and cache folders, replaced when the Oodle library changes, closed by `clear`; `useNativeWorker`); per-request root classes and time budgets in `NativeDecodeRequest` (the clothing preset through the route decoder, `routePresetDecoder`); the graph's `forgetDefaulted` and `readerRuleNotes` (`R11`–`R13`) and `PlannedComponent.readerNotes`; array records read to their end past a short count (`Cr2wDecoder.readPastCount`, reader v3) and relearned defaults; the desktop's bundled `native-decode-worker.js`. A new host read path over untrusted mod files in every preparation (the reader was hardened as R&D, NATIVE-01..25), so a deep review is due. Review focus: the past-count loop's bounds (an element using no bytes stops it; budgets per element), the widened roots per request (only host code sets them), the decoder's lifetime across route reopenings and `clear` (a preparation holding a closed decoder falls back as `internal`), the answer ledger's key and its use by the choice manifests, the transient rule for `unavailable`/`io` fallbacks, `forgetDefaulted`'s path walking on reader-written paths, and the diagnostics' log volume (16 per fetcher). - **Export host** (`src/platform/export/`: product Check, the builder, the product verifier, the manifest reader, the Check runner and `runProductBuild`; `src/platform/api/export.ts`; `src/platform/core/package-plan.ts`; eye makeup's `features/eye-makeup/export/` and `verify/`; host composition `src/compose/exporters.ts`; claude/platform-step8): mod export as products built from feature exporters, one service for both hosts, and the collection's package plan with its actions. - **Runtime bridge batch 3** (`projects/xf-runtime-bridge`, claude/bridge-batch3): new write paths into the running game and a new read path from CET. `cc.open` (write-character, behind `allow_creator_leave`): the redscript safe-moment check (`XFCharacter.OpenRefusal`: phase, combat, vehicle, scene tier, photo mode allowed, the game's save-lock reasons), the save lock taken and confirmed before asking, and the bridge's own `MenuScenario_Idle` event raised through the menu-event blackboard, which switches to the mirror's scenario with an edit tag; the pending request (three-second life, withdrawn on a timeout and by `RestoreAfterKill`); the sequence in `core/Writes.cpp` (`CreatorOpen`). `cc.page` (an added method calling the creator menu's protected `RequestCameraChange`). `cc.apply` by value and by UI slot (`FindValue`, the slot tier of `Find`). `world.time.set` with the creator open. `game.options.read` (read): user settings in script (`XFSettings.Read`) and the engine's render options through `core/OptionsExchange.cpp` and two new natives (`XFBridge_OptionsWanted`, `XFBridge_OptionsReport`) answered by the CET layer's poll. A new host capability (input injection) and new write paths, so a deep review is due before merge. Review focus: every path by which `photo.open` could send a key anywhere but the game window (and the `XFB_NO_INPUT` test guard), the cursor flag's restore on show, hide and kill, the creator-leave gate and the new-game-mode refusal, the framing loop's bounds (clamping, step budget, no unbounded retries), and the package split (presets and `allow_creator_leave` never in the default zip). [README](../../projects/xf-runtime-bridge/README.md), [design §3.6](../runtime/runtime-bridge-design.md#36-autonomy-fewer-player-steps).
- **Runtime bridge phase 2** (`projects/xf-runtime-bridge`, claude/bridge-mcp): a command catalogue and transport-agnostic command API (`tools/api/`), a stdio MCP server on the official SDK (`tools/mcp/`, `tools/mcp-server.ts`), the CLI's `commands`/`run`, a JSON session runner with scripts (`tools/session.ts`, `tools/sessions/`), external capture through `bun:ffi` (`tools/capture/`), and write methods in the plugin (`GameHandlers.cpp`, `core/Params.cpp`) with a redscript actions layer (`redscript/XFRuntimeBridgeActions.reds`) behind `allow_writes`, plus a `-writes` package. A new subsystem outside the Studio, with new write paths into the running game and a new third-party dependency, so a deep review is due before it is used beyond the dedicated test profile. Review focus: the write gate and save lock, `RestoreAfterKill`, parameter checks shared with the self-test host, capture file handling (the `recrop` folder restriction), and MCP input handling. [README](../../projects/xf-runtime-bridge/README.md), [design](../runtime/runtime-bridge-design.md).

An extension of the preview core and the character context rather than a new host capability. A review should look at the reload's timing (deferred until the change that asked for it has finished; never for a released head), that the plate selection can't reach Build or the verifier (a test lists its readers), and the cost of one masculine preparation beside a running feminine one (one at a time; the other waits).
- **Choices prepared ahead** (claude/choice-prefetch): `src/choice-prefetch.ts` (host application service: one job per open Character panel row, batches, a person's own change first, time and disk bounds, per-choice states), `src/choice-manifest.ts` (what a prepared request depended on, so a later session knows it is ready; invalidated by archive identity, winner, ArchiveXL files and WolvenKit), `src/prepared-files.ts` (the prepared files' size, least-recently-used budget and Clear), `warmCharacters` in `src/character-detail-service.ts`, the exporter's `exportAll` (several archives and kinds in one launch) and the fetcher's foreground and background lanes (`src/resolver-host.ts`). A new subsystem and a new host read path (native decoding of one resource), so a deep review is due. Review focus: the package reader's bounds on hostile saves, the saved-hide rule and tag-hiding pass, override application only to body and arms parts (never the head), the resolver cache key with overrides, the native preset read's failure path and cache identity, and the first-use cost of reading every `.xl` factory. [Worn clothing](../../knowledge/clothing.md#6-how-the-studio-draws-worn-clothing). Reviewed at `47f581c` with the body render (PIPE-97..101, PREV-106..108, CORE-92, NATIVE-25, UI-78..79).

A new rendering capability over existing subsystems, not a new host capability; a review should look at the shape transfer's cost on large garments, the helper-joint rule's reach (it binds any flat joint of a driven skeleton within 0.25 m), the texture halving's memory on 8K maps, and that a head part never takes body shapes. [Body rendering](../../knowledge/body-rendering.md). Reviewed at `47f581c` (see Worn clothing).
